- **minhash-database-matching**: Use when assembled plasmid contigs (e.g. Unicycler output FASTA from Plassembler) need to be matched against a known plasmid database to annotate similarity to PLSDB entries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/minhash-database-matching))
- **minhash-gene-cluster-family-redundancy-filtering**: Use when many antiSMASH or gutSMASH-predicted gene clusters (e.g., 1,849 BGCs from 170 S. mutans genomes) must be reduced to a nonredundant representative set before metagenomic read mapping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/minhash-gene-cluster-family-redundancy-filtering))
- **minhash-genome-sketching**: Use when you have paired WGS/FASTQ read files for many metagenomic samples and need a fast MinHash-based all-vs-all distance matrix to test whether samples cluster by a known metadata attribute such as body site. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/minhash-genome-sketching--7f85c303d062d13f))
- **minhash-genome-sketching**: Use when you have many genome assemblies (e.g. 3269 MAG FASTA files) and need to find each one's closest relative in a reference genome set (e.g. 2505 HGR genomes) to assign species labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/minhash-genome-sketching--87b9ee907068d336))
- **minhash-kmer-read-classification**: Use when shotgun reads (FASTA/FASTQ, short or long) from metagenomic or all-food-sequencing (AFS) samples must be taxonomically classified and quantified against massive multi-kingdom reference genome collections faster than alignment-based tools like BWA or MegaBLAST. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/minhash-kmer-read-classification))
- **minhash-kmer-sketching**: Use when building a large reference genome classification database (e.g. multi-kingdom food or RefSeq-scale collections) where storing all k-mers would exhaust memory and a minhash sketch must be computed into partitioned hash tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/minhash-kmer-sketching))
- **minhash-overlap-detection**: Use when building contigs or consensus sequences from long, error-prone nanopore reads without a reference — e.g., to find candidate overlapping reads for greedy contig extension, or to parameterize read overlap search in NanoSpring-style compression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/minhash-overlap-detection))
- **minhash-sketch-distance-estimation**: Use when you need sample-level similarity/distance estimates from raw short-read FASTQ files — e.g. clustering metagenomes, identifying re-sequencings, or selecting Mash sketch/k-mer defaults. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/minhash-sketch-distance-estimation))
- **minhash-sketching-of-gene-clusters**: Use when a metagenomic/metatranscriptomic profiling pipeline receives multiple predicted gene clusters (antiSMASH/gutSMASH GenBank output dirs) that must be deduplicated to avoid ambiguous read mapping downstream. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/minhash-sketching-of-gene-clusters))
- **minhash-sketching-of-sequencing-reads**: Use when you need to identify nanopore reads that overlap a query sequence using k-mer MinHash sketches — e.g., to reproduce or extend NanoSpring's overlap-finding stage for approximate-assembly-based compression of long, error-prone ONT reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/minhash-sketching-of-sequencing-reads))
